============================================================================================================
 LIQUIDITY PARTICIPATION -- order quantity as a share of median daily volume
 Measurement only. Nothing is changed.
============================================================================================================
 Generated 2026-10-15 at commit 41d0606.
 MEDIAN VOLUME IS tradability.median_volume, the one the participation cap
 consumes: rolling(20).median().shift(1) over the trading calendar, reading
 the 20 sessions before the window start, no volume>0 filter, no value until
 a symbol's own file has 20 sessions.
 This file previously used a different median of its own -- last 20 dated
 rows with volume>0, however few -- and the two disagree by more than 1% on
 about a fifth of fills. Figures below are NOT comparable to a copy of this
 file generated before 2026-09-20.
 Fill counts also moved when the universes were repointed at
 Final_Without_Survivorship_Data on 2026-09-18, so both the definition and
 the underlying daily_trades differ from the superseded copy.
 WHICH OF THE TWO MOVED THE NUMBERS, measured 2026-09-20 on the CURRENT
 daily_trades so only the definition varies: on midcap150 (n=1006 fills)
 the old definition gives median 0.058% / p90 0.962% / p99 11.070% / max
 34.458% and the new gives 0.057% / 0.928% / 11.128% / 34.458%, with 12
 fills above 10% either way. The aggregate barely moves. Per FILL the two
 differ by more than 1% on about a fifth of fills, which is why only one
 definition is kept, but it is NOT what changed the headline figures.
 THE 1,614.52% IS GONE BECAUSE ITS DATA IS GONE. That was AIIL SELL
 2021-06-07, 29,465 shares against a 1,825-share median. AIIL's file now
 starts 2024-04-23, so the tree holds no 2021 row for it and no fill of
 that size exists in any current daily_trades. It was not recomputed to a
 smaller number; the run that produced it cannot be reproduced.

  THIS COLUMN IS A ONE-WAY TRIGGER, NOT A FORECAST AND NOT A
  NO-OP PROOF. A non-zero count proves the cell diverges. It cannot
  predict how many binds occur -- after the first bind the book
  differs and later orders are different orders -- and A ZERO DOES
  NOT PROVE A NO-OP: it counts EXECUTED fills, and the cap acts on
  INTENDED orders. nifty100 v1 reads 0 here and binds once, on an
  order research skipped for cash that the cap shrank into
  affordability. Renamed from `fills_over_cap` 2026-09-22.

  THIS IS A DATED MEASUREMENT, NOT A PROPERTY. Capital, universe and
  data all move it. Count the `participation cap` rows in the run's own
  daily_skipped artefact before repeating any claim from this table.
  It answers at today's capital on today's data and says nothing about a
  larger book.
